Faceted filters: keyword, location, work mode (Remote/Hybrid/On-site), job type (Full-time/Part-time/Contract/Internship), level (Entry/Mid/Senior/Lead/Principal), salary range, skills (multi-select from F-84), company, posted-within. Filters are URL-encoded (shareable). Cursor-based pagination (20/page default, 100 max)."},{"sourceLine":1013,"text":"**Ranking** — Default sort: relevance (candidate skill match × job requirements). Weights: 0.45 skill + 0.20 experience + 0.10 location + 0.10 title + 0.10 org_rating + 0.05 recency. Transparency: \\"Why am I seeing this?\\" disclosure on each card."},{"sourceLine":1014,"text":"**Job Detail** — `/jobs/[id]` shows: title, company (linked), location, work mode, salary (min-max with \\"hide pay\\" recruiter toggle), description (sanitized markdown), requirements, benefits, skills required, company overview, similar jobs, apply CTA. If job closed → 410 Gone with \\"similar jobs\\" recommendation."},{"sourceLine":1015,"text":"**Apply** — Multi-step modal: select resume (or attach new), optional cover letter (≤5000 chars), confirm profile completeness (≥70% gate). Autosave draft every 5s to localStorage + `application_drafts` (server). On submit: `job_applications` row created (status `submitted`), `application_status_events` append, `EVT-017 application_submitted`, recruiter notified, candidate receives confirmation."},{"sourceLine":1016,"text":"**Duplicate Prevention** — Partial unique constraint `UNIQUE(applicant_id, job_id) WHERE status NOT IN ('rejected','withdrawn','archived')`. If duplicate → 409 with \\"You've already applied\\" message."},{"sourceLine":1017,"text":"**Saved Jobs** — Candidate saves jobs to `saved_jobs`. View in `/jobs/saved`."},{"sourceLine":1018,"text":"**Saved Searches** — Candidate saves filter combination to `saved_searches`. Sort: relevance, rating, newest, most enrolled, price."},{"sourceLine":1125,"text":"**Course Detail** — `/courses/[id]` shows syllabus preview (public modules/lessons), instructor bio, reviews, prerequisites (from F-84), learning objectives, estimated duration, enrollment CTA."},{"sourceLine":1126,"text":"**Enrollment** — `POST /api/v1/courses/{id}/enroll`. Checks: prerequisites satisfied (F-106), entitlement (free/paid/Pro/Team/institutional license), no active duplicate enrollment. On success: `course_enrollments` row (status `active`), `EVT-006 enrollment_created`, welcome notification."},{"sourceLine":1127,"text":"**Learning Player** — `/courses/[id]/learn` immersive layout: video player (via F-42 media engine), curriculum drawer, lesson checklist, notes panel, Q&A panel, progress bar. Video player supports: play/pause/seek, captions (if provided), transcript, playback speed, PiP, fullscreen, resume-from-last-position."},{"sourceLine":1128,"text":"**Lesson Progress** — Each lesson has content_items (VIDEO, ARTICLE, PDF, QUIZ, ASSIGNMENT). Completion tracked in `module_progress` (idempotent — UNIQUE(enrollment_id, module_id)). Video completion per provider capability."},{"sourceLine":1129,"text":"**Quizzes** — Inline quizzes between lessons. Configurable: time limit, passing score, attempts. Graded quiz contributes to course grade. Results page shows per-question explanations."},{"sourceLine":1130,"text":"**Prerequisites** — Gated by F-106. Attempting to enroll without prerequisites → soft block with \\"Complete X and Y first\\" + link."},{"sourceLine":1131,"text":"**Completion** — Course complete when: all required modules done + final assessment ≥70% (BR-048). Problem statement panel, test cases panel, run button, submit button, timer (if timed challenge)."},{"sourceLine":1187,"text":"**Run (Draft Test)** — `POST /api/v1/challenges/{id}/run` executes against public test cases only. Sandbox execution:"},{"sourceLine":1195,"text":"**Async Judging** — Submission enqueued to `background_jobs` (kind `challenge.judge`). Status transitions: `queued → running → passed\|failed\|error`. Client polls status endpoint every 2s (up to 60s)."},{"sourceLine":1196,"text":"**Scoring** — Score = (passed / total) × difficulty_weight × time_bonus. Normalized to 0–100. Pass threshold: ≥70%."},{"sourceLine":1197,"text":"**XP Award** — On pass: XP awarded (20–100 by difficulty) via idempotent ledger. `UNIQUE(user_id, reference_type='challenge.passed', reference_id=challenge_id)` — one award lifetime."},{"sourceLine":1198,"text":"**Anti-cheat** — Async plagiarism detection (AST comparison against corpus) + behavioral timing analysis. Flags suspicious submissions for review."},{"sourceLine":1199,"text":"**Leaderboard** — Post-MVP. Weekly + all-time tabs."},{"sourceLine":1200,"text":"**Practice Mode** — Post-MVP. Unlimited retries, no XP, hints available."},{"sourceLine":1201,"text":"**AI In-Editor Assist** — Post-MVP (CHALL-013). Provides hints, debugging, explanations inline via embedded Gemini assistant. Public read of public-safe columns.","basis":"Intended SSOT contract; actual per-route enforcement not completely mapped"} | {"source":"SSOT numbered behavior","items":[{"sourceLine":714,"text":"**Profile Auto-creation** — On `auth.users` insert, trigger creates `profiles` row with `id` (FK to auth.users), `email`, `role` (from JWT), `created_at`."},{"sourceLine":715,"text":"**Basic Info Edit** — User navigates to `/profile`. Form fields: `full_name` (required, ≤120 chars), `headline` (≤120), `summary` (≤2000, sanitized markdown), `location` (city-level), `avatar_url` (upload ≤4MB, JPEG/PNG/WebP, MIME sniffed, EXIF stripped), `phone_number` (encrypted at rest AES-256-GCM, masked on public view)."},{"sourceLine":716,"text":"**Skills Management** — User adds skills via typeahead from canonical skills taxonomy (F-84). Each entry: `skill_id`, `proficiency` (Beginner/Intermediate/Advanced/Expert), `years` (optional), `self_reported` (boolean — becomes verified upon platform signal)."},{"sourceLine":717,"text":"**Experience** — CRUD on `profile_experience` rows: `role`, `company`, `start_date`, `end_date` (nullable for current), `description` (≤2000 sanitized markdown), `location`. Company name validated against `organizations` table when possible."},{"sourceLine":718,"text":"**Education** — CRUD on `profile_education`: `institution`, `degree`, `field_of_study`, `start_year`, `end_year`, `grade` (optional). Institution matched against known list when possible."},{"sourceLine":719,"text":"**Certifications** — CRUD on `profile_certifications`: `title`, `issuer`, `issue_date`, `expiry_date` (optional), `credential_id`, `credential_url` (HTTPS-only). Verification status flag."},{"sourceLine":720,"text":"**Portfolio** — CRUD on `profile_portfolios`: `title`, `description`, `demo_url`, `repo_url`, `media_assets` (images/PDFs ≤50MB), `tech_stack` (canonical skills). Public visibility toggle."},{"sourceLine":721,"text":"**Completeness Score** — Recomputed on every edit. Formula: weighted sum of filled sections (basic 20%, skills 15%, experience 20%, education 15%, certifications 10%, portfolio 10%, summary 10%). Score 0–100."},{"sourceLine":722,"text":"**Visibility Toggles** — Per-section control: `public` / `connections_only` / `recruiters_only` / `private`."},{"sourceLine":723,"text":"**Public View** — `/profile/[userId]` renders public-safe columns only. PII columns (`phone_number`, `tax_id`) never exposed."},{"sourceLine":724,"text":"**Data Residency** — `data_residency_region` set at signup from locale (US/EU). Admin write. Instructor/Course Author can propose skill tags (subject to moderation).","basis":"Intended SSOT contract; actual per-route enforcement not completely mapped"} | {"source":"SSOT numbered behavior","items":[{"sourceLine":795,"text":"**Taxonomy Curation (Admin)** — Platform Admin manages the canonical `skills` table: create, edit, deprecate, merge (with redirect map). Each skill: `id`, `canonical_name`, `slug`, `category` (nested ≤3 levels), `description`, `aliases` (array), `status` (active/deprecated), `created_at`. Target: 2,000+ skills at scale."},{"sourceLine":796,"text":"**Skill Relationships (Admin + Computed)** — `skill_relationships` table edges:"},{"sourceLine":802,"text":"**Content Tagging** — Every course, module, lesson, challenge, job, portfolio project, and user skill entry references a canonical `skill_id`. No freeform skill strings allowed (BR-144)."},{"sourceLine":803,"text":"**Market Signal Collection** — Daily job aggregation: `skill_market_signals` table tracks per skill: `demand_index`, `salary_premium`, `growth_rate`, `region`, `window_start`, `window_end`. Aggregated from job postings, salary reports, and hiring outcomes."},{"sourceLine":804,"text":"**Graph Traversal** — Read operations:"},{"sourceLine":809,"text":"**Emerging Skills Detection** — Weekly computation (F-118 dependency): new skill candidates from job postings with `occurrence_count ≥5` in the last 30 days but not in taxonomy → candidate queue for admin review."},{"sourceLine":810,"text":"**Search & Typeahead** — Full-text search over `canonical_name` + `aliases`. Used in profile, course, job, and challenge forms."},{"sourceLine":811,"text":"**Public API** — `GET /api/v1/skills/{id}` returns skill + relationships + market signals (public read). `GET /api/v1/skills/search?q=` for typeahead."},{"sourceLine":812,"text":"**Caching** — Read-heavy; cache hot skills (top 500 by traversal count) in memory with 15-min TTL. Invalidation on taxonomy write."}],"completeness":"Top-level behavior inventory; nested requirements, limits and recovery not fully decomposed or verified"} | See shared route/migration inventories and targeted traces; feature-specific table/async lineage is not fully mapped | If required by approved scope, absence or unverified acceptance blocks that release; beta narrowing requires owner approval. | {"path":"SSOT.md","startLine":780,"endLine":884,"actors":"Platform Admin (taxonomy curation), all users (read), recommendation engine (read), instructors (tag content)","permissions":"Public read. Admin write. Instructor/Course Author can propose skill tags (subject to moderation).","dataDependencies":"`skills`, `skill_relationships`, `skill_market_signals`, `course_skills`, `challenge_skills`, `job_skills`, `profile_skills`, `portfolio_skills`","externalDependencies":"None required. Questions: text, code, design, behavioral. Each has: statement, examples, hidden test cases, time estimate, expected competencies."},{"sourceLine":1356,"text":"**Scheduling** — Invite candidate via `interview_assessments` with: `scheduled_at`, `duration`, `interviewer_id`, `assessment_id`, meeting link (Jitsi/Zoom embed)."},{"sourceLine":1357,"text":"**Pre-Assessment** — Candidate receives: calendar invite (ICS), pre-join device check (mic/camera/browser), instructions. Consent for video recording (opt-in; default off)."},{"sourceLine":1358,"text":"**Live Session** — Video call + collaborative code editor (Monaco) + whiteboard. Interviewer can: assign problem from bank, observe code in real-time, send hints (recorded in transcript), run code against public tests, end session."},{"sourceLine":1359,"text":"**Scoring** — Interviewer completes rubric post-session: technical correctness (1–5), communication (1–5), problem-solving (1–5), code quality (1–5), overall recommendation (Strong Yes / Yes / Neutral / No / Strong No), notes (private)."},{"sourceLine":1360,"text":"**AI Feedback (Optional)** — Post-session analysis: communication signals (speaking pace, filler words, clarity), technical correctness commentary, comparison to benchmark. **Never** emotion/sentiment analysis (prohibited)."},{"sourceLine":1361,"text":"**Recording** — Optional. Requires dual consent. Encrypted at rest. Retention ≤90 days unless explicitly extended. Never used for model training without consent."},{"sourceLine":1362,"text":"**Results** — Candidate receives: pass/fail, high-level feedback, areas for improvement. Interviewer/recruiter see full scorecard."},{"sourceLine":1363,"text":"**Integration with ATS** — Assessment score attached to `job_applications` record. Never exposes full profile or PII."},{"sourceLine":913,"text":"**Credential Revocation** — Admin-only action (academic integrity violation, employer retraction, etc.). Revocation creates a new record with `revoked_at` + `revocation_reason`. Original row retained (append-only). Public URL shows \\"INVALID\\" state. XP clawback logged."},{"sourceLine":914,"text":"**Endorsement Weighting** — When a peer endorses a skill:"},{"sourceLine":919,"text":"**Evidence Display** — On profile: \\"Verified by TalentSphere\\" badge on each skill with ≥1 credential. Click expands evidence list (course, challenge, or endorsement)."},{"sourceLine":920,"text":"**Portable Export** — User can export all credentials as JSON (DSR Art. 20). Export includes verification URLs."},{"sourceLine":921,"text":"**Privacy-Preserving Proof** — Optional privacy-preserving credential presentation. Do not label a hash commitment as zero-knowledge. For each skill: learned → used in job → hired for role. For each assessment: passed → hired → retained."},{"sourceLine":1517,"text":"**Correlation Analysis** — Compute: course completion → hire rate (per cohort), skill acquisition → hire rate (by role), assessment score → performance correlation (where post-hire data available), time-to-competency → success rate."},{"sourceLine":1518,"text":"**Course Quality Score** — Derived score: composite of completion rate, learner satisfaction, hire rate, engagement."},{"sourceLine":1519,"text":"**Instructor Analytics** — Instructor sees: which of their courses lead to hires, learner outcomes (aggregated)."},{"sourceLine":1520,"text":"**Platform Analytics** — Admin sees: overall learning→hiring pipeline, top-performing courses, course recommendations refinement."},{"sourceLine":1521,"text":"**Recommendation Feed** — F-11 (AI assistant) uses correlation data: \\"Learners who took this course were 2.3× more likely to get hired for X role.\\""},{"sourceLine":1522,"text":"**Privacy** — Aggregate-only. Individual learner outcomes never exposed without consent (BR-190)."},{"sourceLine":1523,"text":"**Causation Disclosure** — All outcome claims labelled as correlational (BR-193)."}],"completeness":"Top-level behavior inventory; nested requirements, limits and recovery not fully decomposed or verified"} | See shared route/migration inventories and targeted traces; feature-specific table/async lineage is not fully mapped | No launch promise for this capability; preserve existing working subset and restrict unsafe preview exposure. Identity verification, browser lockdown, AI proctoring."},{"sourceLine":1556,"text":"**Auto-Grading** — MCQ/multi-select auto-graded. Coding via sandbox (F-08). Failed attempts logged."},{"sourceLine":1560,"text":"**Item Analysis** — Post-exam: difficulty index, discrimination index per question. Roles, subfeatures, BR mapping, data lineage and acceptance completeness require explicit review where not covered in the narrative. | {"actors":"Candidate, Recruiter, Hiring Manager","permissions":null,"basis":"Intended SSOT contract; actual per-route enforcement not completely mapped"} | {"source":"SSOT numbered behavior","items":[{"sourceLine":1612,"text":"**Feedback Templates (Admin/Org)** — Platform admins and org admins define templates: stage (screening, interview, offer), reason category (skills gap, experience gap, culture fit, overqualified, position filled, no response, other), structured fields (what went well, what didn't, what to improve), optional freeform note."},{"sourceLine":1613,"text":"**Feedback Capture (Recruiter)** — On stage change (shortlist→reject, interview→reject), recruiter prompted: optional quick feedback via template, or skip (records \\"no feedback provided\\"). Minimum reason category required."},{"sourceLine":1614,"text":"**Feedback Delivery (Candidate)** — On rejection, candidate receives: notification with high-level feedback, link to feedback detail page, aggregate insights, suggested actions (skills to develop, courses to consider)."},{"sourceLine":1615,"text":"**Anonymized Aggregate Insights** — Platform-wide: \\"60% of rejections at this stage cite skills gap\\", \\"Candidates who completed course X were 2.3× more likely to pass screening\\". Only aggregate, never individual."},{"sourceLine":1616,"text":"**Candidate Right to Feedback** — Configurable: Full / Opt-in / Aggregate-only / None."},{"sourceLine":1617,"text":"**AI-Assisted Feedback** — Post-MVP: AI drafts feedback from interviewer notes + stage data. Human (recruiter) reviews and edits before sending. Never sent autonomously."},{"sourceLine":1618,"text":"**Feedback Analytics** — For recruiters: feedback provision rate, feedback timing, candidate satisfaction with feedback."},{"sourceLine":1619,"text":"**Learning Loop** — For candidate: feedback feeds F-91 (Career Readiness) recalculation, suggested courses (F-07) based on skill gaps, optional \\"Would you like to see jobs where you're a stronger fit?\\""}],"completeness":"Top-level behavior inventory; nested requirements, limits and recovery not fully decomposed or verified"} | See shared route/migration inventories and targeted traces; feature-specific table/async lineage is not fully mapped | If required by approved scope, absence or unverified acceptance blocks that release; beta narrowing requires owner approval. | {"path":"SSOT.md","startLine":1599,"endLine":1657,"actors":"Candidate, Recruiter, Hiring Manager","permissions":null,"dataDependencies":"`application_feedback`, `feedback_templates`, `feedback_requests`, `job_applications`, `application_status_events`","externalDependencies":null,"securityRequirements":"RLS own-row for candidates · Org-scoped for recruiters · Feedback immutable once delivered · Audit trail for all feedback","acceptanceSource":["**Acceptance Criteria:** Feedback delivery within 7 days of decision (when provided) · Zero PII leakage in feedback · Aggregate insights k ≥10 always · Candidate can request feedback (30-day window enforced) · Recruiter feedback rate >60% (target metric)"],"ruleReferences":["BR-217","BR-224","SCI-49","SCI-50"],"qualification":"Canonical intended contract extracted; individual clauses and stale cross-references require reconciliation with founder amendments and rule registry. actual per-route enforcement not completely mapped"} | {"source":"SSOT numbered behavior","items":[{"sourceLine":1671,"text":"**Freshness Score Computation** — Nightly job computes for each verified skill: `days_since_verification` (from F-96 credential issue date), `market_demand_trend` (from F-84 market signals), `decay_curve` (per skill category: fast-changing 18-month half-life, moderate 36-month, stable 60-month, foundational minimal), composite `freshness_score` (0–100)."},{"sourceLine":1672,"text":"**Freshness Bands** — Displayed on profile: Fresh (80–100), Current (60–79), Aging (40–59), Stale (20–39), Expired (0–19)."},{"sourceLine":1673,"text":"**Re-verification Prompts** — Triggered when: freshness <60 for a high-demand skill → candidate notified; freshness <40 → recruiter-facing badge shows \\"aging\\"; freshness <20 → skill demoted to \\"self-reported\\" unless re-verified."},{"sourceLine":1674,"text":"**Re-verification Paths** — Multiple ways to refresh: new challenge pass (F-08), new course completion (F-07), new certification (F-115), new project with same skill (F-26), peer endorsement (F-110) if skill is endorsable, self-attestation (weak signal — half credit)."},{"sourceLine":1675,"text":"**Market Drift Alerts** — When a skill's market demand changes significantly: rising → \\"Python is trending up 20% — good time to re-verify\\"; falling → \\"Flash is declining — consider related skills\\"."},{"sourceLine":1676,"text":"**Recruiter Visibility** — On candidate search (F-92), recruiter sees: freshness score per skill, filter by minimum freshness (only fresh skills), preference for fresh candidates in ranking."},{"sourceLine":1677,"text":"**Freshness Analytics** — Platform-wide: average freshness by skill category, re-verification rate, skill half-life accuracy (validated against market data)."}],"completeness":"Top-level behavior inventory; nested requirements, limits and recovery not fully decomposed or verified"} | See shared route/migration inventories and targeted traces; feature-specific table/async lineage is not fully mapped | No launch promise for this capability;
